    @pytest.mark.asyncio
    async def test_input_processing_flow(client: AsyncClient, db_session: AsyncSession):
        # Setup Pyramid
        response = await client.post(
            "/api/v1/pyramids",
            json={"name": "Input Test Pyramid", "description": "For Input Test"}
        )
        assert response.status_code == 201
        pyramid_id = response.json()["data"]["id"]
    
        # 1. Submit Text
        print("Sending text submission...")
        try:
            response = await client.post(
                "/api/v1/contents/text",
                json={
                    "text": "AI is the future.",
                    "title": "Future of AI",
                    "submitter_id": "user123"
                }
            )
            print(f"Text submission response status: {response.status_code}")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Text submission failed with exception: {e}")
            if hasattr(e, 'errors'):
                import json
                print(json.dumps(e.errors(), indent=2, default=str))
            raise e
    
        assert response.status_code == 200
        content_data = response.json()["data"]
        content_id = content_data["id"]

        contributions = response.json()
        # Check if our contribution exists
